Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 S2Dialog 框架，用于从多模态对话库中同时按文本语义和声学对话风格进行对话级语义-风格检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态对话检索方法大多局限于话语级或单模态匹配，难以捕捉整段对话的全局语义连贯性与风格一致性；而对话级检索对于情感对话识别、口语对话系统和对话语音合成等任务具有重要价值。

Method: 提出统一的 S2Dialog 框架，包含对话级文本检索器与对话级声学检索器，分别将文本和声学模态编码为对话级表示；并引入对话级文本-声学对比学习，对齐语义与风格相近的对话、区分无关对话。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集 DailyTalk 上的大量实验表明，S2Dialog 取得了优异的检索性能。

Conclusion: S2Dialog 通过统一的对话级语义-风格检索框架和跨模态对比学习，有效提升了对整段对话的全局语义与风格匹配能力，验证了其在多模态对话检索任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HERMES 是一个可扩展的多智能体框架，利用大型语言模型从超长、非结构化的地学文献（如《无脊椎古生物学论丛》55卷）中自动提取结构化数据，构建了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的FAIR数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地学领域的权威科学知识长期被困于遗留专著和历史文献中，其非结构化文本与复杂版面阻碍了计算化访问，亟需一种可扩展的方法将这些文献转化为机读的结构化数据。

Method: 提出 HERMES 多智能体框架：由一个协调性大语言模型统一组织，将领域约束、验证规则和证据追踪整合到文档级提取流程中，综合处理解析后的文本、表格、图及图注，并对实体和属性开展自动校验与来源追踪。

Result: 系统处理了55卷《无脊椎古生物学论丛》，产出32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库。实体F1约0.90、属性F1约0.91，提取效果在各化石类群间保持稳定；相较人工基线，单卷处理效率提升约6倍。无需额外训练即在古地磁学和地球化学领域验证了跨领域迁移能力。

Conclusion: HERMES 为将历史科学文献转化为面向FAIR的结构化数据提供了可行路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合提供了可持续的基础设施。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了"快速"与"慢速"硅采样（用前沿大模型模拟人类受访者）的算法保真度，发现快速模式在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于慢速模式，但硅采样整体仍处于早期开发阶段，使用时需高度谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样有时能产生令人惊讶的良好人口估计，但作者质疑：快速执行硅采样是否会削弱这种保真度？由此希望评估并扩展硅采样的持续研究，比较快速与慢速两种模式在算法保真度上的差异。

Method: 在一项具有全国代表性的新加坡调查受访者样本上，使用当代前沿大模型，对"快速"与"慢速"两种硅采样模式进行算法保真度比较。重点考察总体均值估计的忠实程度、意见方差的表现，以及人类意见背后的潜在语境空间的扭曲程度。

Result: 硅样品能够对总体均值产生中等忠实度的估计，但仍然低估了意见方差，并扭曲了人类意见背后的潜在语境空间。在这一前提下，快速硅采样模式相对优于传统慢速模式：计算资源和运行时间显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于慢速模式。

Conclusion: 硅采样仍是一种处于早期开发阶段的方法，使用时需高度谨慎。在已知局限性条件下，快速硅采样在效率和算法保真度上均优于慢速硅采样。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 MLC-SLM 挑战赛参赛方案：任务1通过裁剪静音、时间戳校正与EMA微调VibeVoice提升说话人分离与识别；任务2通过合成问答数据与标记直答微调Qwen3-Omni提升对话理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLC-SLM 挑战赛在完整、未分段的多语言对话上评估说话人分离识别与对话语音理解，评估时无真实说话人标签与语句边界，任务2还缺少问答训练集，需要探索有效的训练与数据构造策略。

Method: 任务1采用随机前置静音裁剪、一致性时间戳校正和指数移动平均（EMA）训练策略微调 VibeVoice-ASR-7B；任务2通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构造合成问答对，并微调 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct 进行标记式直接回答。

Result: 任务1评估集上，静音裁剪将 tcpMER 从 18.30% 降至 17.27%，EMA 进一步降至 16.73%；任务2评估集上，联合分布匹配增强与标记直答将准确率从 83.0% 提升至 86.0%。

Conclusion: 针对无分割约束的多语言对话，所提静音裁剪、时间戳校正与EMA策略有效降低分离识别错误率；基于合成数据与标记直答的微调方法显著提升对话语音理解准确率。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对八个法律领域RAG系统在GDPR（英语）与一国国内民法（法语）两个语料库上的幻觉行为进行了细粒度分析，发现幻觉普遍存在：最佳系统不足10%的响应出现幻觉，最差系统接近半数；其中含错误前提的假前提问题幻觉率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法律领域，检索增强生成（RAG）系统若产生无依据的答案将导致严重后果，但缺乏对该领域幻觉问题的系统性和细粒度理解。

Method: 在GDPR（英语）与一国国内民法（法语）两个法律语料库上，对八个法律RAG系统进行细粒度幻觉行为分析；采用主张级（claim-level）与答案级（answer-level）双重评估，报告幻觉密度与严重程度，分析不同问题类别与用户画像下的表现，并在142个法律专家撰写的问题上进行独立验证。

Result: 幻觉现象普遍存在：表现最佳的系统不足10%的响应出现幻觉，最差系统则接近一半；此外，含错误前提、需被拒绝的假前提问题在人工撰写的问题集上产生高幻觉率。

Conclusion: 法律RAG系统仍普遍存在幻觉问题，尤其是假前提问题难以被系统正确识别和拒绝，提示该领域需要在幻觉检测与稳健性方面进一步改进。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaPop 方法，通过结合局部 token 置信度与基于事实流行度的指数（来自外部代理）来自适应调整遗忘压力，并用对偶上升控制器自动平衡遗忘与保留，显著降低了被遗忘内容在复述与对抗性改写下的泄露率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行事实在预训练中被记忆得更深、更难移除，但现有 LLM 遗忘方法对所有训练数据频率一视同仁地施加均匀梯度压力，忽略了流行度差异，导致遗忘效果不佳或过度损害保留能力。

Method: 提出 AdaPop（Adaptive Popularity）方法：将局部 token 置信度与逐事实的流行度相关指数相结合，该指数由外部代理（如 Wikidata 站点链接、LLM-as-Judge）估计；同时采用对偶上升控制器在每个 epoch 自动调整保留惩罚项，实现遗忘-保留平衡的自动化。

Result: 在三个模型家族与两个基准上，AdaPop 在复述查询下比竞品少泄露约 5 倍被遗忘内容，在对抗性改写下少泄露约 1.6 倍。内部指标显示：遗忘集隐藏状态比竞品更远离未遗忘前模型的状态，而保留集表示保持接近。

Conclusion: 针对数据流行度差异进行自适应梯度调整并自动平衡遗忘-保留强度，能显著提升 LLM 遗忘效果，同时更好地保护保留知识的表示。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将长上下文推理教师模型（SU-01）的证明推理能力通过在线策略蒸馏（OPD）迁移到短上下文学生模型，解决分词器不一致、分布失配、回复长度爆炸和训练不稳定等问题，并提出共享文本空间对齐、学生参考KL损失及特殊终止token优势掩码等策略，在多个学生模型上取得数学推理尤其是自然语言数学证明的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏在迁移更强教师模型的推理能力方面很有前景，但将其应用于长上下文教师与短上下文学生时会遇到四个实际挑战：分词器不匹配、教师-学生分布失配、回复长度爆炸以及训练不稳定，制约了推理能力的有效迁移。

Method: 作者以长上下文推理模型 SU-01 为教师、短上下文模型为学生，进行证明推理能力迁移。具体方法包括：(1) 在共享文本空间中进行 OPD，仅对齐学生与教师分词器下占据相同文本跨度的 token，以应对分词器差异；(2) 引入学生参考 KL 损失，并对 </think>、<|im_end|> 等特殊终止 token 的优势进行掩码，以解决过长生成与频繁截断问题。该策略约束学生不过度偏离初始策略，从而缓解分布失配并促进长度平稳增长。

Result: 在同家族与不同家族的学生模型（Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4）上均获得一致的数学推理提升，尤其在自然语言数学证明方面。其中 Intern-S2-Preview 在 ProofBench 上提升 21.2 分，达到 55.2 分，超越 Gemini-2.5-Pro；同时在 HLE、HiPhO 等科学基准上也有提升，表明 OPD 迁移的推理能力可泛化到数学训练域之外。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套面向长上下文教师到短上下文学生的在线策略蒸馏方法，通过共享文本空间 token 对齐、学生参考 KL 损失与特殊终止 token 优势掩码，有效解决了分词器不匹配、分布失配、长度爆炸和训练不稳定等问题，使多类学生模型在数学证明及相关科学基准上取得一致且显著的能力提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Envs-FORGE 是一种面向终端智能体的强化学习环境合成策略，它把验证器奖励转化为逐种子的环境合成动作，通过估计通过率、筛选投影方向并用混合整数线性规划选择动作，生成难易得当且仅含金标准验证的环境，从而在多个基准上显著超越固定配方基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定配方（如 few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对每个种子采用相同的提示策略，无法根据当前智能体策略的需要动态调整任务难度或类型，导致训练环境难易失配、奖励不可靠，限制了终端智能体强化学习的性能提升。

Method: 提出 Envs-FORGE：估计每个种子的通过率；围绕目标学习前沿对六个投影方向动作打分；求解逐种子混合整数线性规划（MILP）选择用于条件化生成的动作；由该动作同步改写指令、夹具、oracle 解、测试与 Docker 环境；仅让通过金标准验证的环境进入 RL 训练。MILP 的索引形式还支持可选的软技能覆盖以实现组合规划。

Result: 在 Qwen 3.5 35B 上，Envs-FORGE 将 Pass@1 在 tb-core 上从 40.0% 提升至 49.2%（+9.2 个百分点），在 tb-2.0 上从 23.0% 提升至 29.4%（+6.4 个百分点），分别超过最强固定配方基线 2.4 和 2.1 个百分点；在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 77.1%，而 Base 为 73.4%。在评估的 4B–35B 模型范围内，tb-core 提升 6.8–9.2 个百分点。所有合成方法均导出 100 个已验证环境，合成 token 在 2.27M–2.88M 之间，保证了下游训练集规模与运行规模一致。

Conclusion: Envs-FORGE 通过将验证器奖励转化为逐种子环境合成动作，能够感知学习前沿并自适应地调整任务难度与类型，在控制训练集规模和算力成本的条件下稳定超越固定配方基线，为终端智能体强化学习提供了更高效的训练环境构建方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型的表征内在维度（ID）如何受数据集词法多样性（末位词元唯一数量）影响，发现存在随尺度变化的两种ID区间，并推导出精确的转折点公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度（ID）被广泛用于探测语言模型的表征复杂度，但目前不清楚ID差异究竟反映语言本身的性质，还是数据集构建方式所造成的人为偏差。作者希望厘清词法多样性这一具体因素对ID估计的影响，以避免对ID的误读。

Method: 论文聚焦分析词法多样性（数据集中唯一末位词元last-token的数量）对ID估计的影响，通过实验考察不同尺度下的ID变化，并推导出一个精确、无参数的公式来计算ID反转发生的临界点，且验证该公式在所有测试尺度下与实际观测的转折点吻合。

Result: 研究发现存在随尺度变化的两种区间的过渡：在低词法多样性时，唯一末位词越少的条件ID越高；而在高词法多样性时次序反转，唯一词越多的条件ID越高。推导出的无参数公式能精确预测每个测试尺度上的反转点。

Conclusion: 一方面，结果提醒人们在把表征集合的内在维度直接解读为复杂度指标时必须谨慎；另一方面，两个ID区间的发现揭示了语言数据在LLM中的一种普遍组织原理，为其内部流形结构提供了新见解。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大型模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）在体育领域的应用，涵盖任务与应用、数据集与基准，以及挑战与未来方向，旨在为体育智能化研究建立基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育近年来在全球范围内热度不断上升，在身体健康、文化交流、社会连接和经济增长中发挥重要作用；与此同时，大型模型尤其是多模态大语言模型的快速发展，展现出重塑体育理解、分析与交互的变革潜力，因此有必要对这一新兴交叉领域进行系统梳理。

Method: 本文采用综述（survey）方法，从三个维度展开：一是概述面向不同参与群体（观众、运动员、教练、裁判、管理者等）的任务与应用；二是详细分析体育相关的数据集与基准；三是对当前挑战与未来方向进行批判性讨论，并提供开源GitHub仓库以汇总相关资源。

Result: 论文构建了一个结构化框架，全面梳理了大型模型在体育领域的任务谱系、参与群体、可用数据集与基准，并识别出现有系统面临的挑战以及未来的研究方向，同时维护了开源资源库Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports。

Conclusion: 大型模型在体育领域展现出广阔的应用前景，但目前仍处于发展初期；论文通过系统综述为大型模型驱动的体育智能研究与实践奠定基础，并指出未来需在数据、基准、跨领域协作与技术落地等方面持续突破。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有摘要评价指标（如 ROUGE、BERTScore 及 LLM-as-judge）无法衡量摘要对具体用户的实用性；本文通过扰动测试和专家人工评估表明，这些指标对信息内容和用户背景（persona）不够敏感，与人类对“信息满足度”的判断一致性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流摘要评价要么关注通用质量，要么关注可读性、事实性等单一属性，却无法度量摘要对个体用户的效用。查询聚焦摘要只捕获部分需求，因为用户很少在查询中完整表达其信息需求；而读者的背景/角色（persona）跨查询相对稳定，能补充缺失的上下文，是判断摘要是否满足读者需求的实用信号。

Method: 作者检验主流摘要指标对信息差异和 persona 差异的敏感度，并设计基本扰动测试来考察这些指标（含强 LLM-as-judge 指标）能否捕捉信息内容的变化；同时开展专家人工评估，基于特定人物的背景与使用场景，衡量其对摘要信息满足度的偏好。

Result: 许多主流指标（包括强 LLM-as-judge 指标）未能通过基本的信息内容扰动测试；传统指标和基于 LLM 的指标均不足以度量信息满足度，且与专家人工判断的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 现有摘要评价指标无法有效衡量面向特定用户（信息需求与 persona 相关）的摘要效用，需要更关注信息满足度这一维度，而不能仅依赖通用质量或属性化指标。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 YOPO（You Only Pass Once）系统，在冻结语言模型单次前向传播中同时完成推理、残差流引导与知识不足时的弃答，解决了引导写入与充分性读取在同一残差流上相互干扰的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上存在两个耦合缺陷：一是未充分利用自身残差流中已编码的证据，二是无法识别输入信息不足而进行臆造（confabulate）。已有两条研究路线分别——条件引导探针在中间层写入残差流、零样本充分性方向读取残差流并弃答——但二者在同一次前向传播中部署会相互干扰：引导写入改变了充分性方向读取的状态，跨域迁移性能最多损失 8 AUROC；若分开两次干净前向传播则推理成本翻倍。

Method: 保持充分性方向不变，训练一个小型网络，在（引导后、干净）成对数据上以均方误差为损失，学习从被引导的残差流重建引导前的残差，无需任何充分性标签；随后在重建结果上读取充分性方向。由此 YOPO 得以在冻结 Qwen2.5（1.5B/3B/7B）主干网络的一次前向传播中同时实现回答、引导与弃答。

Result: 端到端三分类准确率使冻结基线提升一倍以上（1.5B alphaNLI 上由 0.375 升至 0.798），且单次前向传播在所有规模上均超过两次前向传播的参考方案（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863），并在六个模型家族的十个主干网络上得到验证。作者描绘了容量-迁移前沿，量化了"弃答能力不应通过训练注入"这一原则；源侧审计发现自有 alphaNLI 构造泄露了表面伪迹，因此架构性结论锚定在原生标签复现（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）上；在标准四领域套件上贡献了首个"回答或弃答"基准，其中所提门控在全部域内数据集上领先，且无标签充分性方向是唯一能跨域泛化存活的弃答门控家族。

Conclusion: YOPO 通过残差重建网络解耦引导写入与充分性读取，证明可在单次前向传播中融合条件引导与零样本弃答，显著提升推理准确率与弃答可靠性，并确立弃答应为标签无关机制而非训练注入的原则。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Bias Reduction for Local Polynomial Derivative Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13672)
*Fujia Chang,W. John Braun*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代数据锐化方法，用于降低非参数回归中局部线性导数估计的偏差，在保留局部线性拟合简单性的同时，将偏差阶从 O(h²) 降至 O(h^{2l+2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部多项式平滑是非参数回归的常用工具，但局部线性导数估计仍存在 O(h²) 阶偏差。作者希望在不牺牲局部线性拟合简便性的前提下，进一步降低导数估计的偏差。

Method: 方法基于两个期望算子：作用于回归函数的 L₀ 和作用于一阶导数的 L₁。通过反复施加残差算子 R = I − L₀，可构造一系列锐化后的导数估计；经过 l 步锐化后，偏差阶由 O(h²) 降至 O(h^{2l+2})。对高斯核而言，所有锐化系数均为 1，从而得到简洁的单带宽闭式表达式。

Result: 在三个光滑检验函数上的模拟实验表明，该方法能显著降低估计偏差，同时呈现出偏差-方差权衡（bias-variance trade-off）。

Conclusion: 该迭代数据锐化方法可在保持局部线性拟合简单性的同时有效降低导数估计偏差，但偏差的减少伴随方差增大，存在偏差-方差权衡。

Abstract: Local polynomial smoothing is commonly used in non-parametric regression, but local linear derivative estimation still has a bias of order $O(h^2)$. This paper proposes an iterative data sharpening method to reduce the bias of derivative estimates while retaining the simplicity of local linear fitting. The method is based on two expectation operators: $L_0$, acting on the regression function, and $L_1$, acting on the first-order derivative. By repeatedly applying the residual operator $R=I-L_0$, a series of sharpened derivative estimates can be constructed. After $l$ sharpening steps, the bias order can be reduced from $O(h^2)$ to $O(h^{2l+2})$. For the Gaussian kernel, all sharpening coefficients equal 1, giving a simple closed-form single-bandwidth expression. Simulation experiments on three smooth test functions show that this method can significantly reduce the estimation bias while revealing a bias-variance trade-off.

</details>


### [101] [Nonprobability Samples for Small Area Estimation: A Review and Comparative Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13673)
*Sho Kawano,Daniel Vedensky,Qianyu Dong,Ethan Pawl,Qi Wang,Paul A. Parker,Zehang Richard Li,Scott Harold Holan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文综述非概率样本（NPS）最新方法论，以数据缺陷相关性（DDC）作为数据质量度量与方法分类工具，并通过综合模拟研究在不同DDC水平下评估多种NPS方法，还将若干现有方法推广至小区域估计（SAE）场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非概率样本因成本更低、样本量更大且能覆盖传统概率调查难以触及的人群而备受关注；随着传统调查响应率下降，调查研究领域对NPS的兴趣快速增长。小区域估计需要对精细地理尺度和细分人口领域进行估计，NPS方法尤为相关。然而，尽管方法发展迅速，学界对不同条件下哪种方法表现最佳仍缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 作者系统综述了NPS方法的最新进展，引入数据缺陷相关性（DDC）作为衡量数据质量的核心指标，并以其为工具对各类NPS方法进行分类；随后开展了一项全面的模拟研究，在不同DDC水平下对一系列NPS方法进行评估；同时将若干已有方法扩展到小区域估计（SAE）框架中。

Result: 文章给出了基于DDC的NPS方法分类体系，并通过模拟研究展示了不同NPS方法在不同DDC水平下的表现差异，同时给出了若干方法推广至小区域估计的扩展方案，为不同条件下方法的选择提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 通过以DDC作为统一的质量度量与分类框架，并借助大规模模拟研究，本文揭示了各种NPS方法在不同数据缺陷程度下的适用条件，并将方法扩展至小区域估计，有助于研究者根据具体条件选择更合适的NPS方法。

Abstract: Nonprobability samples (NPS) are attractive because they are less costly to collect, can provide substantially larger sample sizes, and may reach populations that traditional probability surveys do not. As response rates for traditional surveys fall, interest in NPS has grown rapidly within the field of survey statistics. These methods are especially relevant for small area estimation (SAE), where there is ever-present demand for estimates at fine geographic scales and detailed demographic domains. Despite rapid methodological development, there remains limited understanding of which approaches perform best under different conditions. In this paper, we review recent developments in NPS methodology, including the concept of data defect correlation (DDC) as a measure of data quality and as a tool for categorizing the various NPS methods. We then present a comprehensive simulation study that evaluates a range of NPS approaches under varying levels of DDC and extend several existing methods to the SAE setting.

</details>


### [102] [Hyperbolic Latent Position Models for Hierarchical Bipartite Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13713)
*Kisung You*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出HELPI模型，将二分二分交互数据的两个对象类型同时嵌入双曲空间，用双曲距离或根节点Gromov积刻画层次化交互，并把几何交互与加性倾向分离。模型具备可识别性与后验收缩性，配套增广变分推断，在Junyi Academy教育数据与美国国会唱名数据上展现了解释性与预测力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分二分数据常同时呈现行/列异质性与层次化交互（如学生答题受先修课程结构约束、议员投票受嵌套政策领域影响）。欧氏空间体积随半径仅多项式增长，无法匹配分支层次结构的指数级扩张；而双曲空间的体积增长与树状结构天然契合，其根节点Gromov积可度量共享祖先深度。因此需要一个能同时容纳层次结构与两类对象异质性的潜变量位置模型。

Method: 提出HELPI（HypErbolic Latent Position model for bipartite Interaction data），将两类对象同时嵌入双曲空间，并用双曲距离或根节点Gromov积作为交互预测项，将该几何项与加性主效应（行、列倾向）明确分离。模型说明：在无约束主效应下，双曲距离预测子与根节点Gromov积预测子在似然意义下等价，径向深度被加性项吸收，从而留下根节点不变的投影层次信号作为可识别的几何目标。随后建立了模型的可识别性与后验收缩理论结果，并针对二分结局开发了增广变分推断算法。

Result: 模拟实验支持层次化交互的恢复，并厘清了弱分支情形下的估计表现。在Junyi Academy教育数据上，以课程锚点获得可解释的几何结构，其在时序预测上接近项目反应理论基准，并能区分“尝试过的练习”与“无条件掌握”。在美国众议院唱名数据上，灵活的HELPI变体优于加性模型与双参数逻辑斯蒂基准，且在拟合时未使用党派标签的情况下恢复出党派与政策结构。

Conclusion: HELPI利用双曲几何实现对二分交互数据中层次化交互的建模，通过将几何信号与加性主效应分离并剥离径向深度，得到一个根节点不变、可识别的投影层次结构。理论与模拟验证了模型的可识别性、后验收缩性与恢复能力，两个真实数据应用表明其在解释性与预测力上兼具优势。

Abstract: Binary bipartite data often combine row and column heterogeneity with hierarchical interaction, as when students answer exercises organized by prerequisites or legislators vote on nested policy areas. Fixed-dimensional Euclidean volume grows only polynomially with radius, whereas branching hierarchies expand exponentially. Hyperbolic space matches this growth, and its rooted Gromov product measures shared ancestry. We propose the \emph{HypErbolic Latent Position model for bipartite Interaction data} (HELPI), which places both object types in hyperbolic space while separating geometric interaction from additive propensities. With unrestricted main effects, a hyperbolic-distance predictor is likelihood-equivalent to a rooted Gromov-product predictor. Radial depth is absorbed by additive terms, leaving a root-invariant projected hierarchy signal as the identified geometric target. We establish identification and posterior contraction results and develop augmented variational procedures for binary outcomes. Simulations support recovery of hierarchical interaction and clarify weak-branch regimes. In Junyi Academy data, curriculum anchors yield an interpretable geometry with temporal prediction close to item-response benchmarks, while distinguishing attempted exercises from unconditional mastery. In U.S. House roll calls, flexible HELPI variants improve on additive and two-parameter logistic benchmarks and recover party and policy structure without using party labels during fitting.

</details>


### [103] [Detection of Structural Distortions in Functional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13762)
*Debanjana Datta,Rituparna Sen,Nalini Ravishanker*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角提出基于状态空间表示的功能时间序列结构变化（结构断裂/范式转变）检测方法，利用分块 Gibbs 采样算法在稀疏、局部化的高维参数空间中准确定位变化位置，并在金融与温度数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维与函数型数据快速增长，函数型数据中的结构变化往往并非全域同时发生，而是局部、稀疏地出现；由于参数空间维度高、单个分量信噪比低，需要跨维度聚合信息才能在全局层面检测变化，而现有方法难以有效处理这种局部化、稀疏且先验未知的结构失真。

Method: 基于函数型时间序列的状态空间表示来刻画结构变化问题，提出多种新颖方法（个体检测与联合/同时检测相结合），并设计高效的分块 Gibbs 采样（Blocked Gibbs Sampling）算法，在贝叶斯框架下精确识别局部结构变化的位置。

Result: 所提出的方法能够准确识别函数型时间序列中局部、稀疏的结构变化位置，并在多个金融和温度数据集上表现出良好的检测效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为函数型时间序列的结构变化检测提供了新的贝叶斯方法体系，通过状态空间建模与分块 Gibbs 采样有效应对了高维、稀疏、局部化的变化模式，实证结果验证了方法在真实数据上的实用性。

Abstract: In the era of modern data science, the rapid proliferation of high-dimensional and functional datasets has fostered increasing interest in the investigation of paradigm shifts and structural breaks.
  Unlike classical univariate time series, structural changes in functional data need not occur simultaneously across the entire domain; instead, they may emerge locally, producing heterogeneous distortions across the underlying functional structure. The patterns of instability often exhibit sparsity, where it is not known \textit{a priori} which specific parameters are undergoing a transition. However, in functional contexts, these shifts are often "localised". The difficulty lies in the high dimensionality of the parameter space, where the signal-to-noise ratio may be low for individual components, necessitating the aggregation of information across dimensions to detect a global change. This paper addresses the problem of detecting structural shifts in a functional time series from a Bayesian perspective. We have developed various novel methodologies that capture the inherent structural distortion in a sequence of random functions, both individually and simultaneously. The formulation of the problem is based on the state-space representation of a functional time series. Efficient Blocked Gibbs Sampling algorithms have been proposed to identify these locations accurately. Further, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on several financial and temperature datasets.

</details>


### [104] [Separating Spatial and Clinical Risk with Node-Splitting SVM Survival Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13847)
*Drew Lazar,Aye Aye Maung*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非参数两阶段方法，先用临床生存树产生临床调整后的累计风险残差，再对残差按空间坐标拟合第二棵树，从而将空间风险与患者临床特征分离，得到分片常数、边界尖锐的空间风险图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恢复生存率的空间变异需要将空间风险与患者临床特征分离，但预后协变量本身具有空间结构，使得这一分离困难。现有平滑贝叶斯方法会平均掉尖锐邻接，而未调整的空间分析会将临床变异误归因于位置。

Method: 采用非参数两阶段方法：第一阶段对协变量拟合临床生存树，利用叶节点的Nelson-Aalen累计风险残差将删失生存结构转换到临床调整尺度；第二阶段对残差按坐标拟合第二棵生存树以恢复空间结构。两阶段均为核偶极分裂生存树，结果空间风险图为分片常数并可捕捉尖锐弯曲边界。

Result: 在乘性脆弱性和外生性条件下，残差给定位置后不含临床协变量，且按脆弱性随机有序。脆弱性Laplace指数的展开量化了残差的近似指数性并识别主导余项。在LeukSurv白血病数据上，该方法与贝叶斯高斯随机场脆弱性在风险升高区域上一致，同时分辨出平滑面平均掉的尖锐邻接；构建连续性并不依赖于第一阶段的特定模型类。模拟中定位了临床与空间贡献无法再分离识别的临界点，平滑基准方法在该点附近同步退化。

Conclusion: 该两阶段非参数方法能有效分离临床特征与空间风险，其残差恢复空间信号的理论依赖于第一阶段的相合性而非具体模型类，因此可推广到其他生存估计器；在实际数据和模拟中均优于平滑基准方法，尤其在尖锐边界和可识别性边界处表现更佳。

Abstract: Recovering geographic variation in survival requires separating spatial risk from patients' clinical characteristics, a problem complicated by prognostic covariates that are themselves spatially structured. We develop a nonparametric two-stage method for this separation. A clinical survival tree fit to the covariates alone supplies leaf Nelson-Aalen cumulative hazard residuals, transferring the censored survival structure to a clinically adjusted scale without imposing a functional form on the clinical hazard, and a second tree fit to these residuals on the coordinates recovers the spatial structure. Both stages are kernel dipole-splitting survival trees, so the resulting spatial risk map is piecewise constant, with sharp, possibly curved boundaries. We establish when the residuals recover the spatial signal: under a multiplicative frailty and an exogeneity condition, they are free of the clinical covariates given location and stochastically ordered by the frailty. An expansion of the frailty Laplace exponent quantifies their approximate exponentiality and identifies the leading remainder. These results assume consistency of the first stage rather than a model class, so the construction extends to other survival estimators. On the LeukSurv leukemia data the method agrees with a Bayesian Gaussian random field frailty about where risk is elevated while resolving sharp adjacencies the smooth surface averages away, and an unadjusted spatial analysis misattributes clinical variation to location. Simulations with known zones, including a sweep through graded violations of exogeneity, locate the point at which the two contributions cease to be separately identifiable, with the smooth benchmark degrading in parallel as that point is approached.

</details>


### [105] [A semiparametric approach for the estimation of covariate-adjusted area under the receiver operating characteristic curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13859)
*Shanshan Liu,Jianlei Huang,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义可加模型的半参数框架，用于估计随协变量变化的及协变量调整后的AUC，可处理二分类与多分类疾病状态，并给出了估计量的大样本性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROC曲线与AUC虽被广泛用于评估生物标志物的判别能力，但在许多临床场景中，诊断准确性会因患者特征不同而显著异质；忽略这种异质性可能得出误导性结论，因此需要能刻画协变量对标志物表现非线性及交互影响的估计方法。

Method: 提出基于广义可加模型（GAM）的半参数框架，估计协变量特定（covariate-specific）与协变量调整（covariate-adjusted）的AUC，允许协变量对生物标志物表现产生非线性和交互效应，并同时支持二分类和多类别疾病状态；进一步建立所提估计量的渐近性质。

Result: 模拟研究显示该方法具有较好的有限样本表现；将方法应用于阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据，观察到生物标志物判别能力在不同协变量之间存在显著异质性。

Conclusion: 所提半参数框架能够灵活刻画协变量对AUC的非线性与交互影响，适用于二分类及多分类结局，并具有良好统计性质，为刻画异质性诊断准确性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Receiver operating characteristic (ROC) and the area under the ROC curve (AUC) are widely used to evaluate the discriminative ability of biomarkers. In many clinical settings, however, diagnostic accuracy varies substantially across patient characteristics, and failure to account for such heterogeneity can lead to misleading conclusions. We propose a new semiparametric framework based on generalized additive models to estimate covariate-specific and covariate-adjusted AUC while allowing for nonlinear and interaction effects of covariates on biomarker performance. Our method accommodates both binary and multicategory disease status. We also establish the asymptotic properties of the proposed estimators. Simulations demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance. We illustrate the method using data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative, where substantial heterogeneity in biomarker discrimination across covariates is observed.

</details>


### [106] [Change-Point Detection for Heterogeneous High-Dimensional Functional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13880)
*Xufei Tang,Dan Zhuang,Houlin Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维函数型面板中稀疏或符号相反的变点信号，作者提出 Energy–PE 统计量，结合个体的平方 CUSUM 能量聚合与广义功效增强项，并辅以基于信息准则的聚类算法估计潜在组结构与变点成员，数值实验和日内股票数据验证其功效与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维函数型面板由跨主体的时序曲线组成，天然存在异质性结构变化。当主体层面的变点信号稀疏或存在符号相反的位移时，传统的均值聚合 CUSUM 过程会因截面平均导致信号衰减或相互抵消，从而明显损失检验功效。

Method: 提出 Energy–PE 统计量：一是将各主体平方 CUSUM 能量进行聚合，以在符号异质的变动下保留个体层面证据；二是加入广义功效增强分量，提升对稀疏弱变点信号的敏感性。在正则条件下建立该统计量的渐近性质。此外，引入潜在组结构和基于信息准则的聚类算法，估计未知组数及各组变点成员归属。

Result: 数值模拟与日内股票应用表明，Energy–PE 能控制检验水平，在稀疏和符号异质的备择假设下提升功效，并提供可解释的检验后汇总结果（组数与成员）。

Conclusion: Energy–PE 统计量有效克服传统均值聚合 CUSUM 在稀疏与符号异质变点下的功效损失，兼具良好的统计性质与实用可解释性。

Abstract: High-dimensional functional panels consist of temporally ordered curves observed across many subjects and naturally exhibit heterogeneous structural changes. Under sparse subject-level break signals or opposite-signed shifts, traditional mean-aggregated CUSUM procedures may suffer noticeable power loss due to signal attenuation or cancellation induced by cross-sectional averaging. We propose a novel Energy--PE statistic, which combines subject-wise squared CUSUM energy aggregation with a generalized power-enhancement component. The energy aggregation preserves subject-level evidence under sign-heterogeneous changes, while the power-enhancement component improves sensitivity to sparse weak break signals. Under regularity conditions, we establish the asymptotic behavior of the proposed statistic. We further incorporate a latent group structure and an information-criterion-based clustering algorithm to estimate the unknown group number and membership for heterogeneous break points. Numerical studies and an intraday stock application demonstrate that Energy--PE controls size, improves power under sparse and sign-heterogeneous alternatives, and yields interpretable post-test summaries.

</details>


### [107] [A Forecast Combination Framework for Hierarchical and Grouped Time Series Reconciliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13886)
*Xixi Li,Zijia Chen,James W. Taylor,Xiaojie Mao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文通过为分层与分组时间序列的预测协调建立预测组合框架，将预测组合与预测协调两个此前相对独立的研究领域连接起来，证明最优组合权重等价于最小迹（MinT）协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测组合与分层/分组时间序列的预测协调长期作为独立领域发展，本文旨在建立二者的联系，为已有协调方法提供新的解释，并为设计新方法提供灵活基础。

Method: 对每个底层序列，从聚合约束中构造最大线性无关的结构化候选预测集；证明组合这些候选预测并聚合底层预测等价于标准无偏线性协调；推导均方误差最优组合权重精确恢复 MinT 协调；提出模块化惩罚框架以支持协方差收缩、权重惩罚和可扩展的分序列独立估计。

Result: 最优权重问题在不同底层序列间可分离，每个底层序列对应一个 Bates–Granger 最优预测组合，从而将 MinT 揭示为一组基于层级诱导候选预测的最优组合；实证表明该框架可实现、具有竞争力，并能在保持一致性的同时提升精度。

Conclusion: 预测组合视角为现有协调方法提供了新的解释，并构成设计新方法的灵活基础；所提模块化惩罚框架嵌套现有 MinT 变体，支持丰富扩展且性能优越。

Abstract: Forecast combining and forecast reconciliation for hierarchical and grouped time series have largely developed as separate research areas. This paper connects the two by developing a forecast combination framework for forecast reconciliation. For each bottom-level series, we construct a maximal linearly independent set of structured candidate forecasts from aggregation constraints, and show that combining these candidates and aggregating the resulting bottom-level forecasts is equivalent to standard unbiased linear reconciliation. Within this representation, we prove that mean-squared-error optimal combination weights exactly recover the widely used Minimum Trace (MinT) reconciliation. We further show that the optimal weight problem is separable across different bottom-level series, each yielding a Bates--Granger optimal forecast combination. This reveals MinT as a collection of optimal combinations over hierarchy-induced candidate forecasts. For finite-sample estimation, we propose a modular penalized framework that nests existing MinT variants and supports rich extensions including covariance shrinkage, weight penalization, and scalable series-wise separate estimation. Empirical results show that the framework is practically implementable, competitive with existing methods, and can improve accuracy while preserving coherence. Overall, the forecast combination perspective offers new interpretations of existing reconciliation approaches and provides a flexible basis for designing new methods.

</details>


### [108] [Semi-supervised Concordance Learning for Optimal Individual Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13945)
*Mengjiao Peng,Yong Zhou,Wenbin Lu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种半监督框架下的最优个体化治疗规则估计方法，利用仅有协变量与治疗信息但无结局标签的大量子样本，通过单指标核平滑插补与协调辅助学习提升估计效率，并验证了其理论性质与实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电子病历等实际场景中，因标注成本高、时间限制与隐私顾虑，仅有极小部分观测具有结局标签，而全部样本的协变量与治疗分配信息可得。已有最优治疗规则方法多为全监督，未能充分利用大量无标签样本，导致估计效率受限。因此作者希望在半监督数据框架下开发更高效、更稳健的最优治疗规则估计方法。

Method: 提出一种半参数推断方法，分两步进行：第一步，利用单指标核平滑（single-index kernel smoothing）对无标签样本的结局进行灵活的非参数插补；第二步，基于协调辅助学习（concordance-assisted learning）估计最优治疗规则。同时建立了估计量的一致性与渐近正态性理论。

Result: 数值模拟表明，在有限样本情形下，所提方法相比全监督估计量具有更高的估计效率和更强的稳健性。通过在 MIMIC-III 与 ACTG175 两个真实数据集上的应用，进一步验证了该半监督框架的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该半监督估计框架能够有效利用无结局标签但含完整协变量与治疗信息的大量子样本，提升最优治疗规则的估计效率与稳健性，在电子病历等半监督场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Finding the optimal individualized treatment rule that maps individual characteristics or contextual information to treatment assignments has been extensively investigated in existing literature, with widespread practical applications. This paper considers the estimation of optimal treatment regimes within a semi-supervised data framework (exemplified by electronic medical record data). In such settings, only a tiny proportion of observations have observed outcome labels, owing to high labeling costs, time limitations, data privacy concerns, and other constraints, while covariates and treatment assignments are available for all study subjects. We develop a semi-parametric inference method for optimal treatment regimes, which leverages outcome- unlabeled samples with complete covariate and treatment information to enhance estimation efficiency. The proposed estimation framework consists of two key steps: first, flexible nonparametric imputation via single-index kernel smoothing; second, subsequent estimation of the optimal treatment regime based on concordance-assisted learning. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of our proposed estimators. Numerical simulation studies demonstrate that our method achieves higher efficiency and stronger robustness relative to fully supervised estimators under finite-sample settings. We further validate the practical value of our proposed framework using the MIMIC-III and ACTG175 datasets.

</details>


### [109] [Interval Estimation of the Common Shape Parameter and Coefficient of Variation of Several Weibull Populations under Progressive Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13971)
*Bankitdor M. Nongrum,Adarsha Kumar Jena*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对多个共享相同形状参数（即相同变异系数）的 Weibull 总体，在渐进 II 型截尾数据情境下，系统比较了多种区间估计方法，并提出最优截尾方案；模拟与实证结果表明贝叶斯方法与方差估计恢复法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weibull 分布在可靠性工程、工业、气象和癌症研究中应用广泛，多个总体共享同一形状参数是统计推断中的常见情形。然而，现有文献对公共形状参数的推断多集中于完全样本，对截尾数据（尤其是渐进 II 型截尾）下的研究不足，因此需要专门探讨该情形下公共形状参数与公共变异系数的区间估计。

Method: 在渐进 II 型截尾下，采用四种频率学派方法——大样本理论、方差估计恢复（Variance Estimates Recovery, VEM）、广义枢轴量（Generalized Pivots）以及贝叶斯推断，对公共形状参数和公共变异系数构造区间估计量并进行比较；同时提出一种最优截尾方案以增强区间估计的稳健性。通过模拟研究与真实碳纤维强度数据实例进行数值比较。

Result: 数值分析结果表明，基于贝叶斯方法与方差估计恢复法构造的区间在性能上表现令人满意，被推荐使用；所提出的最优截尾方案有效提升了区间估计的稳健性。

Conclusion: 针对渐进 II 型截尾下多个 Weibull 总体的公共形状参数与公共变异系数，本文填补了相关研究空白；比较研究表明贝叶斯与方差估计恢复法区间估计性能优越，为实践提供了可靠的推断工具。

Abstract: The Weibull distribution is one of the most flexible continuous probability distributions used to model various failure rates and skewed data in reliability engineering, industry, weather studies and cancer studies. It is a common scenario in statistical inference that several Weibull populations share the same shape parameter, which also implies that they have the same coefficient of variation. While the inferential study of the common shape parameter is often considered for complete samples, the presence of censored data requires a separate investigation that has not received enough attention in the existing literature. Therefore, the focus of this article is on a comparative study of interval estimators for the common shape parameter and the common coefficient of variation under progressive type-II censoring using frequentist methods based on large-sample theory, variance estimates recovery, generalized pivots, and Bayesian inference. An optimal censoring scheme is also proposed to enhance the robustness of interval estimation. Numerical data analyses using a simulation study and a real carbon fiber strength data example are carried out for comparison, and the results recommend the intervals based on Bayesian and variance estimates recovery methods for their satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [110] [Coherence, charity and triangulation in statistical modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13986)
*David J. T. Sumpter*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文借戴维森“经验主义第三教条”批判贝叶斯统计中一系列熟悉二分（频率vs贝叶斯、主客观概率、模型vs数据、先验vs后验），主张信念度本身是客观的，模型与数据是同一信念系统的组成部分，贝叶斯定理描述该系统内部关系而非外部更新；好的统计实践应满足融贯、宽容（善意解释）与三角测量三重约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯统计建立在若干被视为理所当然的二分之上（频率/贝叶斯、主观/客观概率、模型/数据、先验/后验），作者认为这些二分是一种“框架/内容二元论”，会误导我们对统计推理的理解与实践。

Method: 运用戴维森对“经验主义第三教条”的批判（框架/内容二元论），以预测足球队进球频率为贯穿性示例，论证(1)信念度本身即客观；(2)模型与数据应视为同一信念系统的组成部分而非不同种类的事物；(3)贝叶斯定理描述系统内部关系而非外部数据更新模型。进而引入戴维森“彻底解释”的建构纲领，提出信念系统应满足融贯、宽容（善意）、三角测量（对他人与共享世界负责）三重约束。

Result: 三个约束恰好对应良好统计实践（先验的引出、模型核查等）已经在努力满足的要求；显式地谈论这些约束能够改进统计实践。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯统计中熟悉的二分可被理解为应予摒弃的框架/内容二元论；真正重要的是信念系统如何被构建、核查与重建，而融贯、宽容与三角测量正是统计实践应明确遵循并以此自我改进的三重约束。

Abstract: Bayesian statistics rests on a few familiar distinctions: frequentist vs. Bayesian, objective versus subjective probability, a model versus the data it is fitted to, a prior versus a posterior. Here, I use Donald Davidson's "third dogma of empiricism" to critique such distinctions in terms of scheme/content dualisms. With a single running example -- estimating how often a football (soccer) team scores -- I argue that a degree of belief is in itself objective, that model and data are better seen as parts of one belief system than as different kinds of things, and that Bayes' theorem describes relations within that system rather than data updating a model from outside. What matters instead is how belief systems are built, checked and rebuilt. To this end, I use Davidson's constructive programme of radical interpretation to suggest that a belief system should be not only coherent (as statisticians already require) but also charitable and triangulated, answerable to other people and to a shared world. I argue that these three constraints are exactly what good statistical practice -- from eliciting priors to checking models -- already tries to satisfy and talking about them explicitly could improve this practice.

</details>


### [111] [Handling covariate shift by model averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14025)
*Yifan Zhang,Tianfa Xie,Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对协变量偏移下的目标分布预测问题，本文提出一种自适应重要性加权模型平均方法，通过将密度比取不同幂次构造候选最小二乘估计器族，并对其实施基于数据驱动的凸组合平均，以平衡偏差与方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源分布上拟合的方法在目标分布上可能并非最优，而标准的密度比重要性加权虽然能纠正分布错配，但少量过大的密度比值会在有限样本中大幅放大估计方差，形成偏差-方差权衡。

Method: 将重要性加权校正的程度视为模型不确定性来源：对估计的密度比取一定范围的幂指数，构造一族自适应重要性加权最小二乘估计器（端点分别对应普通最小二乘与标准重要性加权最小二乘），并在这些候选估计器上构建数据驱动的凸组合平均。

Result: 在模型误设定下，所提估计器相对不可行的最优凸组合具有渐近最优性；在正确设定下，发散惩罚可使所选权重集中于普通最小二乘端点。模拟与实证应用表明该方法在所考虑场景下取得具有竞争力的目标预测性能。

Conclusion: 该自适应重要性加权模型平均方法通过灵活的密度比幂次与数据驱动加权，有效缓解协变量偏移下的偏差-方差权衡，在理论和实证层面均表现良好。

Abstract: Distributional mismatch between the data used to construct a statistical procedure and the population to which it is ultimately applied is pervasive in modern data analysis. We study covariate shift, a fundamental instance of this problem, and develop an adaptive importance-weighted model averaging method for prediction when labeled observations are available from a source distribution, whereas only unlabeled covariates are observed from the target distribution. Procedures fitted directly to the source sample generally optimize prediction risk under the source distribution and may therefore be suboptimal for target prediction. Importance weighting by the density ratio between the target and source covariate marginals provides a natural correction, but a small number of large density-ratio values can substantially inflate the variance of the resulting estimator in finite samples. We address this bias-variance trade-off by treating the degree of importance-weighting correction as a source of model uncertainty. Specifically, we construct a family of adaptive importance-weighted least-squares estimators by raising the estimated density ratio to a range of exponents, with the endpoints corresponding to ordinary least squares and standard importance-weighted least squares, and form a data-driven average over these candidates. Under model misspecification, the proposed model averaging estimator is shown to be asymptotically optimal relative to the infeasible best convex combination of the candidate estimators. Under correct specification, a diverging penalty is shown to make the selected weights concentrate near the ordinary least-squares endpoint. Simulations and a real-data application show that the proposed method achieves competitive target-prediction performance across the settings considered.

</details>


### [112] [A Unified Bayesian Model for Voter Turnout Estimation: Combining Surveys, Aggregate Data, and Selection Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14062)
*Margus Niitsoo,Reimo Rebane,Tarmo Jüristo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯分层框架，通过联合整合个体层面调查数据、官方选民投票率边际和人口普查单元计数，来估计人口子群体在小区域层面的投票率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的小区域投票率估计对政治分析至关重要，但方法上面临挑战：调查数据存在过度报告、非代表性和不可忽略的无应答问题；基于聚合数据的生态推断（EI）则易受生态谬误影响。因此需要联合多源数据的统计方法。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯分层框架，包含三个模型：（1）多层次生态推断（EI）模型，将MRP式结构扩展到聚合数据；（2）投票与边际（PM）模型，在一个统一似然中联合拟合调查数据与官方边际；（3）完全选择（FS）模型，在随机接触假设下加入Heckman式选择修正，并使用信息先验进行识别。

Result: 在基于经验人口普查人口特征、且含受控选择机制的模拟中，FS模型显著优于现有基准，尤其在强选择偏差或严重删失情况下表现突出，但对未建模的选择噪声敏感。在2023年爱沙尼亚议会选举的应用中，PM模型在可用的验证边际上与当前最先进方法表现相当，FS模型则显示出依赖于信息先验的适度改进。

Conclusion: 联合调查、官方边际和人口普查数据的贝叶斯分层方法，尤其是加入选择修正的FS模型，能有效提升存在选择偏差时的小区域投票率估计，但其改进幅度依赖于信息先验的设定。

Abstract: Accurate small-area estimation of voter turnout for demographic subgroups is crucial for political analysis but methodologically challenging. Survey data suffer from over-reporting, non-representativeness, and non-ignorable non-response, while ecological inference (EI) from aggregate data is vulnerable to the ecological fallacy. We propose a Bayesian hierarchical framework that jointly integrates individual-level survey data, official aggregate turnout margins, and census cell counts. The framework comprises three models: a multilevel ecological inference (EI) model that extends MRP-style structure to aggregate data, a Poll-and-Margin (PM) model that jointly fits survey and margins in one coherent likelihood, and a Full Selection (FS) model that adds a Heckman-style selection correction under a random-contact assumption and uses informative priors for identification. In simulations with controlled selection mechanisms based on empirical census demographics, FS substantially improves over established benchmarks, particularly under strong selection bias or heavy censoring, but is sensitive to unmodeled selection noise. In an application to the 2023 Estonian parliamentary election, PM performs similarly to the current state of the art on available validation margins, with FS showing a modest improvement that depends on the informative priors.

</details>


### [113] [Generation-Powered Inference for Distribution-Valued Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14542)
*Yijiao Zhang,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出"生成驱动推断"(GPI)框架,利用辅助生成模型改进对分布值参数(如Wasserstein重心)的统计推断,通过函数值桥表示将非线性Wasserstein空间推断转化为Hilbert空间均值函数估计,实现类似预测驱动推断的最优信息借用,并给出渐近理论与置信带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型越来越多地输出分布值结果(如单细胞基因组学中预测的细胞对遗传扰动的响应),但这些模型本身并不完美。作者希望开发一套统计方法,既能利用生成模型的预测信息提高推断效率,又不过度依赖模型正确性,从而在模型误设时仍保持稳健。

Method: 提出生成驱动推断(GPI)的通用框架,聚焦Wasserstein重心及相关分布泛函。核心创新是引入函数值桥表示(function-valued bridge representation),将Wasserstein空间中的非线性推断转化为Hilbert空间中均值函数的估计,从而构建类似预测驱动推断的增广估计框架。基于此发展了具有最优信息借用性质的一族GPI估计量,并建立了相合性、渐近正态性与同时置信带,以及对线性泛函和Wasserstein距离的有效推断方法。

Result: 模拟研究表明,GPI估计量相比仅用标注数据的方法具有效率增益,且在生成模型误设情况下表现稳健。在K562细胞的Perturb-seq研究中,利用State基础模型生成的合成扰动响应,改进对40S核糖体模块扰动相关通路级共识基因表达分布的推断。

Conclusion: 生成驱动推断为利用不完美生成模型辅助分布值参数推断提供了统一且理论上严谨的框架,通过桥表示将Wasserstein空间问题线性化到Hilbert空间,实现最优信息借用和稳健推断,并在真实单细胞数据中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Modern generative models increasingly produce distribution-valued outputs, such as predicted cellular responses to genetic perturbations in single-cell genomics. While these models provide valuable auxiliary information, they are inherently imperfect, creating a need for statistical methods that leverage their predictions without relying on their correctness. We propose generation-powered inference (GPI), a general framework for improving inference on distribution-valued parameters using auxiliary generative models. Focusing on Wasserstein barycenters and related distributional functionals, we introduce a function-valued bridge representation that transforms inference in the nonlinear Wasserstein space into estimation of a mean function in a Hilbert space, enabling an augmented estimation framework analogous to prediction-powered inference. We develop a family of GPI estimators with optimal information borrowing, establish consistency, asymptotic normality, and simultaneous confidence bands, and derive valid inference for linear functionals and Wasserstein distances. Simulation studies demonstrate efficiency gains over labeled-data-only methods and robust performance under generative model misspecification. We illustrate the proposed framework using a Perturb-seq study of K562 cells, where synthetic perturbation responses generated by the State foundation model are used to improve inference for pathway-level consensus gene expression distributions associated with perturbations of the 40S ribosome module.

</details>


### [114] [An integration of decision trees into latent class modeling with covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14091)
*Johan Lyrvall,Felix Clouth*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将决策树拟合到潜在类别的新方法，旨在解决传统潜在类别分析中逻辑模型在存在复杂协变量交互时易误设、解释困难的难题，并以二值协变量决策树为例进行阐述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在类别分析的既有方法论文献主要依赖逻辑模型对给定协变量下的类别隶属关系建模。但该方法在协变量间存在复杂交互作用时具有重要缺陷：逻辑模型容易因遗漏交互项而被错误设定，且纳入高阶交互项会急剧增加解释的复杂性。因此需要一种能直接建模协变量效应、且易于解释的方法。

Method: 作者提出将决策树建模直接整合进潜在类别分析框架，以易于解释的树结构引导视线穿过预测变量的组合，最终得到条件分类。该方法无需对带协变量的潜在类别模型引入非传统假设，基于成熟的模型估计流程，可便捷地在现有软件中实现。本文聚焦于二值协变量的决策树，提出了两种树剪枝策略，并给出真实数据示例。

Result: 论证了所提方法在建模二值协变量时的可行性与可解释性，通过真实数据示例展示了其应用效果，并描述了两种树剪枝策略。

Conclusion: 该方法的最终目标是开创潜在类别分析方法论文献中的新研究方向，并通过这一新型数据分析工具推动应用研究的进展。重要扩展方向包括：将方法推广到多项和连续协变量（通过开发更高级的预测变量内部分裂程序），以及开发与评估替代性树剪枝策略。

Abstract: We propose a novel methodology for fitting decision trees to latent classes. The latent class analysis methodological literature has previously been focusing on logistic models of class membership given covariates, which has important drawbacks in the presence of complex interactions between covariates: logistic models are easily misspecified by omitting some interaction terms and complexity of interpretation increases rapidly with inclusion of higher-order interaction terms. Our proposed approach directly integrates decision tree modeling into the latent class analysis framework to model the covariate effects as an easily interpretable tree leading the eye through combinations of predictors to a final conditional classification. The novel methodology does not require any non-traditional assumptions for latent class models with covariates, is based on well-established routines of model estimation, and can be readily implemented in existing software. In the present paper, we focus on decision trees for binary covariates. We present the proposed approach, describe two tree pruning strategies, and provide a real-data illustration. Important extensions include generalizing the approach to multinomial and continuous covariates by developing more advanced within-predictor splitting procedures, and developing and evaluating alternative tree pruning strategies. The ultimate aim of this work is to initiate a novel research line in the latent class analysis methodological literature, and to facilitate the advancement of applied research via a novel data analytical tool.

</details>


### [115] [FastJM: An R Package for Efficient Implementation of Semiparametric Joint Models for Longitudinal and Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14127)
*Shanpeng Li,Emily Ouyang,Ace Isabel Mejia-Sanchez,Xinping Cui,Gang Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍 R 包 FastJM，为三类半参数联合模型提供计算高效、统一的接口，涵盖模型设定、估计、推断、可视化、动态预测及预测性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合模型能灵活刻画纵向过程与时间-事件过程的关联，在生物医学研究中应用广泛；但对大规模和复杂生物医学数据，其拟合存在较大的计算负担，成为应用瓶颈。

Method: FastJM 在期望最大化（EM）框架内，采用定制化的线性扫描算法高效更新非参数基线风险函数，解决半参数联合建模的主要计算瓶颈；并结合地标式多元联合建模框架支持常用的时变潜在关联结构。该包支持三类模型：单纵向生物标志物联合模型、多纵向生物标志物联合模型，以及具有异质组内变异的单纵向生物标志物联合模型。

Result: FastJM 提供统一的接口，实现模型设定、估计、推断、可视化和动态预测，并支持预测性能评估，包括交叉验证的时变准确度指标与时间无关的一致性统计量；文中通过可复现示例展示了其核心功能。

Conclusion: FastJM 通过线性扫描算法显著提升了半参数联合模型的计算效率，为大规模复杂生物医学数据提供了高效、统一且功能完整的联合建模软件工具。

Abstract: Joint models provide a flexible framework for characterizing the association between longitudinal and time-to-event processes and have been widely applied in biomedical research. However, fitting joint models can be computationally challenging for large-scale and complex biomedical data. This paper introduces the \proglang{R} package \pkg{FastJM}, which provides computationally efficient frequentist estimation for three classes of semiparametric joint models: joint models with a single longitudinal biomarker, joint models with multiple longitudinal biomarkers, and joint models with a single longitudinal biomarker with heterogeneous within-subject (WS) variability. Within an expectation--maximization framework, \pkg{FastJM} employs customized linear-scan algorithms to efficiently update the nonparametric baseline hazards, thereby addressing a major computational bottleneck in semiparametric joint modeling. The package also supports commonly used time-dependent latent association structures by integrating these algorithms with a landmark multivariate joint modeling framework. \pkg{FastJM} provides a unified interface for model specification, estimation, inference, visualization, dynamic prediction, and prediction performance assessment, including cross-validated time-dependent accuracy measures and time-independent concordance statistics. We describe the underlying methodology and software implementation and demonstrate the main functionality of \pkg{FastJM} through reproducible examples.

</details>


### [116] [Exact Likelihood Inference for Snowball-Sampled Erdős-Rényi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14129)
*Nurzhan Sapargali,Sergio Buttazzo,G\''oran Kauermann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对通过滚雪球抽样等链接追踪设计获得的网络数据，本文给出了当总体为Erdős–Rényi图且采用全邻域招募的r波滚雪球抽样时，边概率π的精确似然及其最大似然估计，并构建了基于精确抽样分布的置信区间，显著降低了推断偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过链接追踪设计（如滚雪球抽样）收集网络数据时，抽样机制依赖于分析所试图估计的网络结构本身；若把观测样本误当作完整网络，推断会产生严重偏倚。该选择问题在一般情况下难以处理，作者希望找到可精确求解的设定。

Method: 作者证明该选择问题在特定条件下存在精确解：总体为Erdős–Rényi图，采用全邻域招募（full-neighbourhood recruitment）的r波滚雪球抽样。他们推导出样本的精确似然，证明它构成关于边概率π的弯曲指数族，并具有低维充分统计量；在此基础上得到正确考虑抽样设计的π的最大似然估计，该估计充分利用了最小充分统计量中的信息。置信区间则通过对基于精确抽样分布的检验进行反演构造，其中分布通过蒙特卡洛模拟近似。

Result: 模拟研究表明，与朴素估计相比，该校正方法大幅降低了偏倚；即使样本仅覆盖网络的0.1%，估计仍基本无偏。此外，在一系列边概率和波数组合下，所构造的置信区间在蒙特卡洛误差范围内达到名义覆盖水平。

Conclusion: 对于从Erdős–Rényi总体中抽取的全邻域r波滚雪球样本，可以精确刻画其抽样分布并构造出正确考虑抽样设计的估计与置信区间，从而有效解决链接追踪设计导致的推断偏倚问题；该方法在极小样本覆盖比例下仍然表现良好。

Abstract: Network data obtained through link-tracing designs, such as snowball sampling, are collected through a mechanism that depends on the very structure the analysis seeks to estimate. Ignoring this dependence and treating the observed sample as though it were itself a complete network can lead to substantially biased inference. While the resulting selection problem is intractable in general, we show that it admits an exact solution for $r$-wave snowball samples, with full-neighbourhood recruitment, drawn from an Erdős--Rényi population. We derive the exact likelihood of such a sample and show that it defines a curved exponential family in the edge probability $π$, with a low-dimensional sufficient statistic. Building on this result, we obtain the maximum likelihood estimator of $π$ that correctly accounts for the sampling design and, as a function of the minimal sufficient statistic, makes full use of the information in the sample. Simulation studies show that this correction substantially reduces bias relative to the naive estimator, remaining effectively unbiased even when the sample covers as little as 0.1\% of the network. We further construct valid confidence intervals for $π$ by inverting a test built on the exact sampling distribution, approximated via Monte Carlo simulation. Simulation studies confirm that these confidence intervals attain the nominal coverage level within Monte Carlo error across a range of edge probabilities and numbers of waves.

</details>


### [117] [Change Point Detection and Localization in High-Dimensional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14344)
*Patrick Bastian,Daria Tieplova,Nina Dörnemann,Tim Kutta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出高维时间序列变点检测的新推断工具，涵盖顺序（在线）变点检验与多变化点的回顾式定位（带全局受控置信区间），并给出高维 Hölder-Gauss 逼近理论及仿真与加州野火空气污染的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时间序列中变化往往是稀疏且异步发生的，传统基于低维（固定维数）的方法难以有效检测；同时需要同时支持在线数据流的顺序检验与离线多变化点定位，并要求置信区间具有全局受控的错误水平。

Method: 检验统计量基于最大范数（maximum norm）构造，以对稀疏、异步变化获得功效；两类问题均采用相关的多尺度（multiscale）统计量，在不同分辨率水平上搜索变化。理论验证上，将固定维数下的传统 Hölder 不变性原理推广为高维类似物——Hölder-Gauss 逼近，可粗略理解为高维部分和过程 Hölder 范数的高斯逼近。

Result: 仿真研究表明该方法具有良好有限样本表现；在加州野火空气污染数据的应用中，能够检测到测量站面板中异步发生的变化，验证了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 所提多尺度最大范数统计量与高维 Hölder-Gauss 逼近理论为高维时间序列变点检测提供了统一且有效的推断框架，且该逼近工具可推广至变点检测之外的问题（如高维平稳性检验）。

Abstract: We present new inference tools for change point detection in high-dimensional time series. We discuss two distinct statistical applications: First, sequential change point testing in an incoming data-stream. Second, retrospective localization of multiple changes, with confidence intervals at a globally controlled error level. Test statistics are built on the maximum norm to generate power against sparse and asynchronous changes. Both problems are tackled by related multiscale statistics that search for changes in the data at many different levels of resolution. For fixed dimension, our statistical approaches can be validated using traditional Hölderian invariance principles. In this paper, we present the high-dimensional analogue: Hölder-Gauss-approximations, which can be (roughly) interpreted as the Gaussian approximation for a Hölder-norm of the high-dimensional partial sum process. Such approximations are of interest beyond change point detection and can be used for other problems such as for stationarity testing in high dimensions.
  We evaluate finite-sample performance in a simulation study and give an application to air contamination due to wildfires in California, which occurs asynchronously across a panel of measuring stations.

</details>


### [118] [Joint Estimation of Sparse Multilayer Networks via Graph Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14536)
*Youngseok Song,Sofia C. Olhede*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图极限（graphon）的非参数联合估计方法——多层网络直方图（multi-network histogram），用于对共享同一顶点集的多层网络（multiplex）数据进行联合建模，在理论上给出加权均方积分误差上界与最优带宽，并证明联合建模能降低误差、支持更小带宽，从而实现对稀疏层的高分辨率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络数据常涉及共享同一组个体的多种交互类型；由于各层共享顶点，单层结构可借助其他层信息得到更好解释，因此需要联合建模来刻画多层交互的生成机制。同时，各层可能存在不同的稀疏程度与连接结构，需通过共享潜变量刻画层间异质性。

Method: 将多层网络观测建模为由一组带缩放（scaled）的图极限（graphon）所构成，并基于块模型（blockmodel）近似构建非参数联合估计量，即"多层网络直方图"（multi-network histogram）。该框架通过在所有层间共享的潜变量来刻画异质性，同时捕捉各层不同的稀疏性和连接结构。

Result: 建立了多层网络直方图的理论性质：给出加权均方积分误差（weighted MISE）的上界，并推导出使该误差最小化的最优带宽。结果表明，通过跨层信息共享，联合建模能够降低估计误差并允许更小的最优带宽，从而即使在更稀疏的层中也能实现高分辨率估计。仿真研究与印度村庄社会经济网络的实证应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 共享顶点的多层网络可通过联合建模显著提升估计精度与分辨率，尤其对稀疏层效果明显；所提出的多层网络直方图具有理论保证和实际可用性，为非参数多层网络分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Network datasets in modern applications often involve multiple types of interactions occurring over a shared set of individuals. Characterizing the generating mechanisms of these interactions can be enhanced by joint modelling, as shared vertices allow layers to help explain the structure of other layers. We model multiplex observations using graph limits, called a scaled set of graphons, and develop a nonparametric joint estimator based on blockmodel approximations, termed the multi-network histogram. This nonparametric framework captures each layer's varying sparsity and connection structure, accounting for heterogeneity via shared latent variables across all layers. We establish the theoretical properties of the multi-network histogram, providing an upper bound for the weighted mean integrated squared error and deriving the optimal bandwidth that minimizes this error. By leveraging information across layers, this joint modelling achieves a reduction in error and a smaller optimal bandwidth, which enables high-resolution estimation even in sparser layers. Its usefulness is demonstrated through simulation studies and an application to socioeconomic networks in an Indian village.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629)
*Oscar L. Cruz-Gonzalez,Valérie Deplano,Badih Ghattas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出一种改进的多输入多输出物理信息深度算子网络（M3PI-DeepONet），通过聚合注入策略和逐层门控机制，仅用0.3%内部标注数据即可快速预测腹主动脉瘤（AAA）非定常3D流场，速度误差<4%、压力误差约5%，推理速度较CFD快约36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上可执行的、患者特异性的血流动力学评估（壁面剪切应力、涡旋结构与压力分布）对判断腹主动脉瘤的风险性演化至关重要，但传统计算流体动力学（CFD）计算成本高、难以实时。现有PI-DeepONet在处理复杂3D流动时存在架构瓶颈，需要一种能同时融合多物理约束并具有输入自适应能力的新架构。

Method: 提出M3PI-DeepONet，核心是"聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）"策略：将多输入分支的潜在表征融合后再注入主干（trunk），使坐标基能适配多重物理约束。该架构首次将逐层门控机制与多分支算子网络拓扑结合，形成输入自适应主干基。模型集成3D Navier-Stokes方程作为物理控制律，基于物理信息残差、初始与边界条件训练，仅使用0.3%的标注内部数据及选定的分支条件信号，同时预测非定常3D速度场与压力场。

Result: 在理想化AAA几何上，M3PI-DeepONet同时预测非定常3D流速与压力场，平均相对L2速度误差低于4%，压力误差约5%；在分支条件输入可用的情况下，相比参考CFD模拟实现约36倍的保守cycle推理加速。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了深度学习在心血管疾病建模中的应用，向实时、非侵入式临床诊断迈出一步，证明多分支聚合注入与门控机制结合的物理信息算子网络能有效应对复杂3D血流预测。

Abstract: Clinically actionable, patient-specific hemodynamic assessment, specifically wall shear stress, vortex structure and pressure distributions, is critical for determining risky or unfavorable evolution in Abdominal Aortic Aneurysms (AAA). While Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) show promising results in complementing established 5 tools such as Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), a persistent architectural challenge remains for complex 3D flows. In this direction, we propose a Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets (M3PI-DeepONet) designed for predicting unsteady flows in an idealized AAA geometry. Central to our model is the Aggregated Injection strategy, where latent representations from multiple input branches are fused prior to trunk injection, allowing the coordinate basis to adapt to multiple physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first architecture to combine the layer-wise gating mechanism with a multi-branch operator-network topology, yielding an input-adaptive trunk basis. Additionally, we integrate the 3D Navier-Stokes equations as governing physical laws, so the model is trained based on physics-informed residuals, initial and boundary conditions, and only 0.3% of the labeled internal data together with the selected branch-conditioning signals. The M3PI-DeepONet simultaneously predicts unsteady 3D flow velocity and pressure fields with an average relative L2 velocity error below 4% and pressure error around 5% while achieving a conservative retained-cycle inference speedup of approximately 36x compared to reference CFD simulations once the branch inputs used for conditioning are available. This work advances the application of deep learning in cardiovascular disease modeling, marking step toward real-time, non-invasive clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [120] [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Christopher P. Muller,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了最大似然估计（MLE）在训练高斯过程（GP）用于概率回归/分类时的脆弱性，对比了理论基础更强的度量指标，并提出实用改进方案；实验表明这些方案能提升下游贝叶斯优化等设计任务的效果，其准确率、不确定性量化与推理成本甚至可超越表格基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE 作为最流行、直观的 ML 训练机制，在其前提假设不满足时会导致模型泛化能力变差；这种脆弱性同样影响被广泛认为"抗过拟合"的高斯过程。工程设计中 GP 大量使用，因此需要从根本上评估 MLE 的脆弱性并提供更稳健的替代方案。

Method: 本文以概率回归和分类任务为背景，将 MLE 与理论基础更强的统计度量指标进行系统性对比，并提出实用的训练/选择损失函数的改进方案；通过在贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中开展大规模实验加以验证，并与表格基础模型（如 TabPFN）比较预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本。

Result: 研究表明 MLE 在假设不成立时表现脆弱；所提出的替代方案在广泛实验中表现更稳健，在下游贝叶斯优化任务中有效，并且构建的 GP 在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本上可超越表格基础模型。

Conclusion: MLE 训练 GP 具有脆弱性，工程实践者应采用理论基础更可靠的损失函数选择与训练策略，以获得准确、鲁棒的 GP，并兼顾下游设计任务性能与推理效率；相关代码已开源，可作为实践蓝图。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become an indispensable part of modern engineering design workflows. A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting. In this paper, we fundamentally evaluate the brittleness of MLE in the context of training GPs for probabilistic regression or classification tasks. We compare theoretically grounded metrics against MLE and propose practical solutions. Our extensive studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our solutions in downstream design tasks such as Bayesian optimization and provide a blueprint for practitioners to build accurate and robust GPs that can even outperform tabular foundation models in terms of prediction accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and inference cost. Our contributions are publicly available via GitHub at https://github.com/Bostanabad-Research-Group/GP-vs-TabPFN-vs-GPyTorch.

</details>


### [121] [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937)
*Yicheng Kang,Yuling Jiao,Xin Geng,Mahesh Nagarajan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出MODERN框架，一个基于深度学习（Inception残差网络）的智能制造质量监控与故障隔离系统，包括缺陷概率控制图、缺陷区域估计器、小样本迁移监控技术及假设检验评估方法，并建立了估计与诊断的minimax最优收敛率，得出升级监控设备未必总是有益的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造系统部署大量传感器、成像设备和计算机，为跨模块即时通信和智能管理提供了条件，但现有工业质量控制实践尚未充分利用这些增强能力进行高效的质量监控与故障隔离。

Method: 提出MODERN深度学习框架：(1) 基于Inception残差神经网络架构构建控制图，监控产品含缺陷的似然率；(2) 利用迁移学习开发缺陷区域估计器以定位缺陷位置；(3) 针对训练数据不足的情况提出仅需小样本的迁移监控技术，并用假设检验方法定量评估方法适用性；(4) 理论上建立缺陷似然估计与故障诊断的minimax最优收敛率。

Result: 理论上证明了缺陷似然估计和故障诊断均达到minimax最优收敛率；实证上通过模拟实验和真实数据，证明MODERN方法优于当前最先进方法；并得出一个看似反直觉的管理启示——制造商不一定应不计成本地持续升级其监控设备。

Conclusion: MODERN框架将深度学习和迁移学习能力有效整合进工业质量控制，在监控精度、故障定位和小样本场景下均表现优越，同时揭示设备升级存在边际收益递减，为制造商设备投资决策提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Smart manufacturing processes are often installed with a large number of sensors, imaging devices and computers, which not only enable instant communication across various modules of a production system but also aid in intelligent manufacturing management. In this paper, we introduce MODERN, a deep learning framework for quality monitoring and fault isolation, which integrates these enhanced capabilities into the practice of industrial quality control. Using the architecture of an inception residual neural network, we develop a control chart that monitors the likelihood of a product containing defects. We also propose a faulty region estimator that identifies the defective area using transfer learning. To extend our framework to cases where there are not sufficient training data, we suggest a transfer monitoring technique that requires only a small sample size and a hypothesis testing approach for quantitatively assessing the applicability of our method. Theoretically, we establish the minimax optimal convergence rate for both our defect likelihood estimation and fault diagnosis. Our results lead to a seemingly counter-intuitive managerial implication - it may not always be in a manufacturer's best interests to keep upgrading its monitoring equipment regardless of the cost. Empirically, we demonstrate the superior performance of our method in comparison with a state-of-the-art approach using both simulated experiments and real data.

</details>


### [122] [Extending Occam's inversion with lasso fusion, overcomplete dictionaries, and isotropic total variation regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14225)
*Anandaroop Ray*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将 l1 正则化反演统一到 Occam 反演框架下，厘清了多维全变分（TV）的两种形式（仅各向同性形式对边缘方向不变）、lasso 融合的合成/分析两种视角，并系统比较了坐标下降、IRLS 与 split Bregman 三种求解器，给出 1D 与 2D 问题的使用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Occam 反演稳健但常用 l2 模型粗糙度导致模型过平滑；l1 正则化可得到视觉上更锐利的模型，但地球物理学中的 l1 反演与统计学/成像文献存在脱节——例如二维差分算子的全变分存在各向异性与各向同性两种不同形式，其差异常被忽略，导致反演结果依赖网格边缘方向。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，将 l1 反演以熟悉的 Occam 形式呈现，鼓励地球物理学家采用。

Method: 在 Occam 框架下（l2 数据范数 + l1 模型正则化），作者证明一维情形下 lasso 融合的合成（synthesis）与分析（analysis）两种途径归结为同一问题；并将其推广到多维及小波变换等字典算子。系统阐述坐标下降、迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）与 split Bregman 三种求解器的数学原理，用于求解线性问题（1D 回归、2D 去模糊）与非线性问题（1D 航空瞬变电磁，含野外数据实例）。

Result: 一维 lasso 融合的合成与分析两种形式等价；多维权变分需区分各向异性（非方向不变）与各向同性（对边缘方向不变）形式。数值与野外实例表明：1D 问题推荐坐标下降；2D 问题推荐 IRLS 优于 split Bregman，IRLS 所需的最小二乘求解次数最多可少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文成功将 l1 正则化反演的各种形式统一为地球物理学家熟悉的 Occam 反演，澄清了多维全变分的正确形式，并通过三个求解器的对比给出实用建议（1D 用坐标下降、2D 用 IRLS），有望推动 l1 正则化反演在地球物理领域的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Occam's inversion is a robust algorithm to perform nonlinear geophysical inversion. It provides the smoothest model within observation noise, thereby discouraging geological overinterpretation. While Occam originally penalised l2 model roughness, l1 can be used to provide models that are visually sharp. However, l1 regularised geophysical inversion has diverged from the larger body of statistics and imaging literature. For example, l1 regularisation with a difference operator (i.e., total variation) in multiple dimensions has two distinct forms, only one of which is invariant to edge orientation -- a distinction often overlooked in geophysics. In one dimension this reduces to the fused lasso problem in statistics. Within the Occam framework, for l2 data norm and l1 model regularisation, we show that lasso fusion through either synthesis or analysis leads to the same 1D problem.
